Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the extraction object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 593. The moduli space has dimension at most 898 with 3 connected components.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the extraction space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 593 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 898. QED.
